````json
  "content": "&lt;project_context>\n# 项目: faith-evidence-ie\n\n## 运行环境\n- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x\n- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account=\"root\"）\n- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP\n- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige\n\n\n## 环境搭建\n连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。\n\n## 输出风格\n回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。\n&lt;/project_context>\n## 任务：备份 flatnli_r3 checkpoint → Kill → 修复 NLI device → 重启\n\n**严格按序执行，每步确认后才进下一步。**\n\n### 背景\nflatnli_r3 (PID 53292, cuda:3) 正在正常训练，但 NLI 推理在 CPU 上导致 ~100s/step（应为 ~12s/step）。需要 kill 后修复代码让 NLI 跑在 GPU 上，然后从 checkpoint 恢复。\n\n训练进度约 step ~95/756，save_steps=20，save_total_limit=5，所以现有 checkpoint 大约是最近 5 个 20 步间隔的。\n\n### Step A: 备份 checkpoint（Kill 之前）\n\n1. 找到 flatnli_r3 的 output 目录：\n```bash\n# 可能的路径\nls -d /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_flatnli_r3/ 2>/dev/null\nls -d /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_s2_flatnli_r3/ 2>/dev/null\n# 或者从 trainer_state.json 找\nfind /root/autodl-tmp -name \"trainer_state.json\" -path \"*flatnli*\" 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n2. 列出所有 checkpoint：\n```bash\nls -d &lt;output_dir>/checkpoint-* | sort -t- -k2 -n\n```\n\n3. 备份所有现有 checkpoint：\n```bash\nmkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_backup/flatnli_r3_healthy/\ncp -r &lt;output_dir>/checkpoint-* /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_backup/flatnli_r3_healthy/\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_backup/flatnli_r3_healthy/\n```\n\n4. **确认备份完成**再进入下一步。\n\n### Step B: Kill flatnli_r3\n\n```bash\n# 杀实际训练进程（PID 53292）及其子进程\nkill -- -$(ps -o pgid= -p 53292 | tr -d ' ') 2>/dev/null || kill 53292\nsleep 3\nps -p 53292 -o pid= 2>/dev/null && echo \"STILL_ALIVE\" || echo \"KILLED\"\nnvidia-smi  # 确认 cuda:3 释放\n```\n\n### Step C: 修复 NLI device\n\n找到 grpo_trainer.py 中 NLI model 的 device 设置。关键参数是 `--reward_device`。\n\n1. 先确认当前代码中 reward_device 的默认值和使用方式：\n```bash\ngrep -n \"reward_device\" /root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/grpo_trainer.py\n```\n\n2. 检查 `--reward_device` 参数是否已存在。flatnli_r3 的启动命令是：\n```\npython -m freige.training.grpo_trainer --stage 2 --kl_coef 0.0 --num_iterations 1 --save_steps 20 --format_reward_weight 0.3 --f1_reward_weight 3.0 --ced_reward_weight 1.0 --lr 5e-5 --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 --num_generations 4\n```\n注意没有 `--reward_device` 参数，默认可能是 CPU。\n\n3. 如果 `--reward_device` 参数存在但默认是 CPU，重启时加 `--reward_device cuda` 即可。\n如果 NLI device 是硬编码的，需要修改代码。\n\n### Step D: 重启 flatnli_r4\n\n使用 `submit_training_job` 提交（**不要** 用 nohup/tmux 手动启动）。\n\n重启命令（注意 bash -c 包裹）：\n```\nbash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.grpo_trainer --stage 2 --kl_coef 0.0 --num_iterations 1 --save_steps 20 --format_reward_weight 0.3 --f1_reward_weight 3.0 --ced_reward_weight 1.0 --lr 5e-5 --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 --num_generations 4 --reward_device cuda --save_total_limit 40 --resume_from_checkpoint &lt;best_checkpoint_path>\"\n```\n\n关键修改点：\n- `--reward_device cuda`（核心修复）\n- `--save_total_limit 40`（保留更多 checkpoint）\n- `--resume_from_checkpoint`：用最新/最大 step 的备份 checkpoint\n\n提交参数：\n- server: rtx5090-4x\n- gpu_ids: [\"cuda:3\"]\n- exp_id: mvp2_grpo_flatnli_r4\n- project: faith-evidence-ie\n\n### Step E: 验证重启\n\n等 2-3 个 step 后检查：\n1. `step_time < 20s`（确认 NLI 在 GPU）\n2. `nvidia-smi` 确认 cuda:3 有两个进程（训练 + NLI model）\n3. `ps -p &lt;new_PID>` 确认进程存活\n4. 检查 log 看 reward 计算正常\n\n### 输出\n汇报每一步的结果：\n- 备份了哪些 checkpoint（文件列表）\n- Kill 确认\n- NLI device 修复方式（参数还是代码改动）\n- 新实验 PID 和 step_time\n- nvidia-smi 截图\n\n### 服务器\n- SSH endpoint: rtx5090-4x\n- 环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 项目路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/`"
````

&lt;project_context>
# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：备份 flatnli_r3 checkpoint → Kill → 修复 NLI device → 重启

**严格按序执行，每步确认后才进下一步。**

### 背景
flatnli_r3 (PID 53292, cuda:3) 正在正常训练，但 NLI 推理在 CPU 上导致 ~100s/step（应为 ~12s/step）。需要 kill 后修复代码让 NLI 跑在 GPU 上，然后从 checkpoint 恢复。

训练进度约 step ~95/756，save_steps=20，save_total_limit=5，所以现有 checkpoint 大约是最近 5 个 20 步间隔的。

### Step A: 备份 checkpoint（Kill 之前）

1. 找到 flatnli_r3 的 output 目录：
```bash
# 可能的路径
ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_flatnli_r3/ 2>/dev/null
ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_s2_flatnli_r3/ 2>/dev/null
# 或者从 trainer_state.json 找
find /root/autodl-tmp -name "trainer_state.json" -path "*flatnli*" 2>/dev/null
```

2. 列出所有 checkpoint：
```bash
ls -d &lt;output_dir>/checkpoint-* | sort -t- -k2 -n
```

3. 备份所有现有 checkpoint：
```bash
mkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_backup/flatnli_r3_healthy/
cp -r &lt;output_dir>/checkpoint-* /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_backup/flatnli_r3_healthy/
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_backup/flatnli_r3_healthy/
```

4. **确认备份完成**再进入下一步。

### Step B: Kill flatnli_r3

```bash
# 杀实际训练进程（PID 53292）及其子进程
kill -- -$(ps -o pgid= -p 53292 | tr -d ' ') 2>/dev/null || kill 53292
sleep 3
ps -p 53292 -o pid= 2>/dev/null && echo "STILL_ALIVE" || echo "KILLED"
nvidia-smi  # 确认 cuda:3 释放
```

### Step C: 修复 NLI device

找到 grpo_trainer.py 中 NLI model 的 device 设置。关键参数是 `--reward_device`。

1. 先确认当前代码中 reward_device 的默认值和使用方式：
```bash
grep -n "reward_device" /root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/grpo_trainer.py
```

2. 检查 `--reward_device` 参数是否已存在。flatnli_r3 的启动命令是：
```
python -m freige.training.grpo_trainer --stage 2 --kl_coef 0.0 --num_iterations 1 --save_steps 20 --format_reward_weight 0.3 --f1_reward_weight 3.0 --ced_reward_weight 1.0 --lr 5e-5 --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 --num_generations 4
```
注意没有 `--reward_device` 参数，默认可能是 CPU。

3. 如果 `--reward_device` 参数存在但默认是 CPU，重启时加 `--reward_device cuda` 即可。
如果 NLI device 是硬编码的，需要修改代码。

### Step D: 重启 flatnli_r4

使用 `submit_training_job` 提交（**不要** 用 nohup/tmux 手动启动）。

重启命令（注意 bash -c 包裹）：
```
bash -c "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.grpo_trainer --stage 2 --kl_coef 0.0 --num_iterations 1 --save_steps 20 --format_reward_weight 0.3 --f1_reward_weight 3.0 --ced_reward_weight 1.0 --lr 5e-5 --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 --num_generations 4 --reward_device cuda --save_total_limit 40 --resume_from_checkpoint &lt;best_checkpoint_path>"
```

关键修改点：
- `--reward_device cuda`（核心修复）
- `--save_total_limit 40`（保留更多 checkpoint）
- `--resume_from_checkpoint`：用最新/最大 step 的备份 checkpoint

提交参数：
- server: rtx5090-4x
- gpu_ids: ["cuda:3"]
- exp_id: mvp2_grpo_flatnli_r4
- project: faith-evidence-ie

### Step E: 验证重启

等 2-3 个 step 后检查：
1. `step_time < 20s`（确认 NLI 在 GPU）
2. `nvidia-smi` 确认 cuda:3 有两个进程（训练 + NLI model）
3. `ps -p &lt;new_PID>` 确认进程存活
4. 检查 log 看 reward 计算正常

### 输出
汇报每一步的结果：
- 备份了哪些 checkpoint（文件列表）
- Kill 确认
- NLI device 修复方式（参数还是代码改动）
- 新实验 PID 和 step_time
- nvidia-smi 截图

### 服务器
- SSH endpoint: rtx5090-4x
- 环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 项目路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/`

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The trainer calls `trainer.train()` at line 510 without resume_from_checkpoint. HF Trainer supports `trainer.train(resume_from_checkpoint=path)`, but the custom script doesn't expose that as a CLI arg.

I have two options:
1. Add `--resume_from_checkpoint` as an argparse argument and pass it to `trainer.train()`
2. Use HF Trainer's auto-resume feature: if `output_dir` contains checkpoints, HF Trainer can automatically resume from the latest one

For option 2, I can copy the checkpoint-100 into the new output_dir, or point output_dir to the old one. 

The cleanest approach is to add the CLI argument. Now I need to check the argparse section around lines 330-380 and the train call at line 510 to implement this properly.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"GRPO 训练脚本: 基于 TRL GRPOTrainer 的多阶段 curriculum 训练。\\n\\n支持单卡和多卡（DeepSpeed ZeRO-2/3）训练，默认 4-bit QLoRA。\\n\\n单卡:   python -m freige.training.grpo_trainer --stage 1 --output_dir ./grpo_s1\\n多卡:   accelerate launch --config_file configs/accelerate_4gpu.yaml \\\\\\n            -m freige.training.grpo_trainer --deepspeed configs/deepspeed_zero2.json \\\\\\n            --stage 1 --reward_device cpu\\n不量化: 加 --no_quantize\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Optional\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import Dataset\\nfrom peft import LoraConfig, PeftModel, TaskType\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig, TrainerCallback\\nfrom trl import GRPOConfig, GRPOTrainer\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nclass KLDivergenceCallback(TrainerCallback):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Monitor KL divergence — log only, no early stopping.\\n\\n    With QLoRA 4-bit, KL between ref and policy is unreliable due to\\n    quantization noise. We log it for diagnostics but do not penalize or stop.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, max_kl: float = 100.0):\\n        self.max_kl = max_kl\\n\\n    def on_log(self, args, state, control, logs=None, **kwargs):\\n        if logs and \\\"kl\\\" in logs:\\n            kl = logs[\\\"kl\\\"]\\n            if kl > self.max_kl:\\n                logger.warning(\\\"KL divergence %.2f exceeds monitoring threshold %.1f (monitoring only, not stopping)\\\", kl, self.max_kl)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences \\\"\\n    \\\"and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. For each triple, provide the \\\"\\n    \\\"head entity, relation type, tail entity, and the sentence IDs that serve as evidence. \\\"\\n    \\\"Output as a JSON list.\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_json_output(text: str) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"从模型输出中解��� JSON 列表。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    try:\\n        return json.loads(text)\\n    except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n        start = text.find(\\\"[\\\")\\n        end = text.rfind(\\\"]\\\") + 1\\n        if start >= 0 and end > start:\\n            try:\\n                return json.loads(text[start:end])\\n            except OK, so PID 67045 is actually the bash wrapper, not the python process itself. Looking at the log more carefully:
```

```text
There are two phases of log output visible:
1. First phase (from PID 66504's failed run) - the HF connection failures, but then...
2. Wait, actually the log was overwritten by the new run (using `>` redirect). Let me read more carefully.

Looking at the timestamps:
- `23:29:11` - still retrying for tokenizer_config.json
- `23:29:21` - now retrying for config.json (tokenizer loaded from cache after retries)


- `23:29:27` - NLI model starts loading
- `23:29:31` - NLI model successfully loaded with entailment index 1, and stage 2 training begins with the flat_nli reward and tau schedule
- `23:29:33` - GRPO training kicks off and the progress bar shows it's actually running

But there's something odd here - I'm seeing retry failures for config.json continuing through `23:29:32`, which overlaps with when training supposedly started.

Looking more carefully at the logs, I think what happened is the second run appended to the same log file, and the null bytes scattered throughout are leftover tqdm output from the first failed attempt. The actual successful run is the one with those clean log entries at `23:29:27` through `23:29:33` - that's when the NLI model loaded, stage 2 initialized, and training actually began.

But there's something odd here: the progress bar shows `0/756 [00:00<?, ?it/s]`, which means it's starting from step 0, not resuming from step 100 like it should be. The `resume_from_checkpoint` parameter was supposed to load from `/root/autodl-tmp/grpo_flatnli_r3/checkpoint-100`, but since the new output directory is empty, I'm wondering if the checkpoint loading actually worked or if it just started fresh.

Actually, HF Trainer typically shows `0/756` initially and then jumps to the correct step once it loads the optimizer state, so I should wait a bit longer to see if that happens. The retry failures in the logs are probably just from the initial HEAD request before falling back to cache—the NLI model loaded fine from cache at 23:29:31, so that's not a real blocker.

The null bytes and out-of-order timestamps in the log are likely from tqdm progress bar output getting mixed in, which happens when using `>` redirect. I should wait a couple of minutes and check the logs again to see the actual training progress and step timing.
```

</details>

### Assistant

_source line 225 · 2026-05-14T15:30:51.453Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The key data point is: `13%|█▎ | 101/756 [00:56<06:09, 1.77it/s]`

This tells us:
- Training resumed from step 100 (showing 101/756 which is the first step after resume)
- **101 steps in 56 seconds** — wait no, it says `101/756 [00:56<06:09, 1.77it/s]`, which means it's at step 101 out of 756, elapsed time 56s. But since it resumed from checkpoint-100, the first step (100→101) took about 56s.


- The progress bar started when training resumed, so the 56 seconds includes checkpoint loading time. The speed of 1.77 it/s seems suspiciously fast for GRPO training with generation and NLI reward computation — that would be less than 0.6 seconds per step, which doesn't quite add up.

- I should account for the fact that each tqdm step might represent an optimizer step (16 accumulation steps), not individual generation steps, which would make the timing more reasonable. The first step also likely includes compilation warmup overhead.

- I need to wait for more data to get a stable estimate of actual training speed rather than relying on this initial measurement.
```
